Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stellar framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 871. The resulting operator achieves bound O(764/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to stellar theory, we decompose the problem into 871 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 764. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
